````text
**NUE = Non University Examination (Examination conducted at school level)

** Building Design and Drawing - course is program specific & practical exam needs to be conducted.

Environmental Science - course is for the general awareness about the environment.

Community Service - course provides students a real-life hands on experience in the community and sensitizes them towards the societal challenges like poverty, illiteracy, hunger, child labor and inequality.

# Complex Variables and Transforms - Syllabus is common for B Tech (EXTC, Mechanical, Civil and Mechatronics) and MBA Tech (Mechanical), can use common question paper.

# Management Accounting for Engineers - Syllabus is common for B Tech (Mechanical, Civil and Computer), MBA Tech (Mechanical, Computer, Data Science and Al) and B Tech Integrated (EXTC) can use common question paper.
````

````text
SVKM's Narsee Monjee Institute of Management Studies
Mukesh Patel School of Technology Management & Engineering

Institute Vision and Mission

Vision:

Play a distinct role in providing excellence in engineering and management education
thereby creating human resources of value to industry and society both at national and
international level.

Mission:
e Formulate relevant curriculum through strong industry linkages and interaction.

e Ensure quality of education through pedagogical innovations

e Undertake and promote relevant research

e Ensure multifaceted development of students, faculty and staff through continuous
introspection and inputs.

e Set up the international linkages with Institutes /industry of repute.

CIVIL Department Vision and Mission

Vision:

Create competent Civil Engineering professionals with sound technical and management
credentials with an ability to take part in sustainable development with global ensign for
the benefit of the society.

Mission:

e Mt: Provide quality education in conformity with advancements in technology and
management

e M2: Encourage relevant research, development and entrepreneurship qualities in
students, faculty and staff through teamwork with perpetual learning

e Ms3: Develop strong industry institute linkages to ascertain and resolve the socio-
economic problems with due consideration to safety and economy in ethical manner

Program Educational Objectives (PEOs)

The objectives of the Civil Engineering Program are to develop Civil Engineering
graduates who are prepared to:

e PEOI: Practice the profession of Civil Engineering with competence, professionalism
and competitiveness in various facets like design, construction and project
management

e PEO2: Pursue personal and professional growth with superior work ethics

e PEOS3: Pursue advanced studies and research at post graduate level

BTECH/CIVIL/2017-18/SCHEME/ Page 1 of 9
--- page 2 ---
SVKM's Narsee Monjee Institute of Management Studies
Mukesh Patel School of Technology Management & Engineering

Program Outcome

The outcomes of the Civil Engineering Program are

e PO1: Apply the knowledge of mathematics, science, engineering fundamentals for the
solution of complex engineering problems in general and civil engineering problems in
particular.

e PO 2: Identify, formulate, research literature, and analyses complex civil engineering
problems reaching substantiated conclusions using first principles of mathematics,
natural sciences and engineering sciences.

e PO 3: Design solutions for complex engineering problems and design system
components or processes that meet the specified needs with appropriate consideration
for public health and safety, and cultural, societal and environmental considerations.

e PO 4: Use research-based knowledge and research-methods including design of
experiments, analysis and interpretation of data, and synthesis of the information to
provide valid conclusions to civil engineering problems.

e PO 5: Create, select, and apply appropriate techniques, resources and modern
engineering and IT tools for civil engineering challenges.

° PO6: Apply reasoning informed by the contextual knowledge to assess societal, health,
safety, legal and cultural issues and the consequent responsibilities relevant to civil
engineering practice.

° PO 7: Understand the impact of the professional engineering solutions in societal and
environmental contexts, and demonstrate the knowledge of, and need for sustainable
development. ,

e¢ PO 8: Apply ethical principles and commit to professional ethics and responsibilities
and norms of civil engineering practice.

e PO 9: Function effectively as an individual, and as member or leader in diverse teams,
and in multidisciplinary settings.

e PO 10: Capacity to communicate effectively on complex engineering activities with the
engineering community and with the society at large.

e¢ PO 11: Skills to demonstrate knowledge and understanding of the engineering and
management principles and apply these to one's own work.

e PO 12: Background to recognize the need for, and have the preparation and ability to
engage in independent and lifelong-learning in the broad context of technological
change.

e PO 13: Perceive a civil engineering project from its conception to execution along with
the inputs required for such a project.
````
